  wscript.exe "%MINIAI_HIDE_VBS%" "%~f0"

  exit /b 0
)
set "MINIAI_UI=%TEMP%\MiniAI_Installer_UI_%RANDOM%%RANDOM%.ps1"
powershell.exe -NoLogo -NoProfile -WindowStyle Hidden -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -EncodedCommand JABFAHIAcgBvAHIAQQBjAHQAaQBvAG4AUAByAGUAZgBlAHIAZQBuAGMAZQA9ACcAUwB0AG8AcAAnAAoAJABzAGMAcgBpAHAAdABQAGEAdABoAD0AJABlAG4AdgA6AE0ASQBOAEkAQQBJAF8AUwBFAEwARgAKACQAdQBpAFAAYQB0AGgAPQAkAGUAbgB2ADoATQBJAE4ASQBBAEkAXwBVAEkACgAkAHMAbwB1AHIAYwBlAFQAZQB4AHQAPQBbAEkATwAuAEYAaQBsAGUAXQA6ADoAUgBlAGEAZABBAGwAbABUAGUAeAB0ACgAJABzAGMAcgBpAHAAdABQAGEAdABoACwAWwBUAGUAeAB0AC4ARQBuAGMAbwBkAGkAbgBnAF0AOgA6AFUAVABGADgAKQAKACQAcwB0AGEAcgB0AE0AYQByAGsAZQByAD0AJwBfAF8ATQBJAE4ASQBBAEkAXwBVAEkAXwBCAEUARwBJAE4AXwA1AEYAOQBBADIAMgBfAF8AJwAKACQAZQBuAGQATQBhAHIAawBlAHIAPQAnAF8AXwBNAEkATgBJAEEASQBfAFUASQBfAEUATgBEAF8ANQBGADkAQQAyADIAXwBfACcACgAkAHMAdABhAHIAdABJAG4AZABlAHgAPQAkAHMAbwB1AHIAYwBlAFQAZQB4AHQALgBJAG4AZABlAHgATwBmACgAJABzAHQAYQByAHQATQBhAHIAawBlAHIALABbAFMAdAByAGkAbgBnAEMAbwBtAHAAYQByAGkAcwBvAG4AXQA6ADoATwByAGQAaQBuAGEAbAApAAoAJABlAG4AZABJAG4AZABlAHgAPQAkAHMAbwB1AHIAYwBlAFQAZQB4AHQALgBJAG4AZABlAHgATwBmACgAJABlAG4AZABNAGEAcgBrAGUAcgAsAFsAUwB0AHIAaQBuAGcAQwBvAG0AcABhAHIAaQBzAG8AbgBdADoAOgBPAHIAZABpAG4AYQBsACkACgBpAGYAKAAkAHMAdABhAHIAdABJAG4AZABlAHgAIAAtAGwAdAAgADAAIAAtAG8AcgAgACQAZQBuAGQASQBuAGQAZQB4ACAALQBsAHQAIAAkAHMAdABhAHIAdABJAG4AZABlAHgAKQB7AHQAaAByAG8AdwAgACcASQBuAHMAdABhAGwAbABlAHIAIABVAEkAIABtAGEAcgBrAGUAcgBzACAAYQByAGUAIABtAGkAcwBzAGkAbgBnAC4AJwB9AAoAJAB1AGkAVABlAHgAdAA9ACQAcwBvAHUAcgBjAGUAVABlAHgAdAAuAFMAdQBiAHMAdAByAGkAbgBnACgAJABzAHQAYQByAHQASQBuAGQAZQB4ACsAJABzAHQAYQByAHQATQBhAHIAawBlAHIALgBMAGUAbgBnAHQAaAAsACQAZQBuAGQASQBuAGQAZQB4AC0AJABzAHQAYQByAHQASQBuAGQAZQB4AC0AJABzAHQAYQByAHQATQBhAHIAawBlAHIALgBMAGUAbgBnAHQAaAApAAoAWwBJAE8ALgBGAGkAbABlAF0AOgA6AFcAcgBpAHQAZQBBAGwAbABUAGUAeAB0ACgAJAB1AGkAUABhAHQAaAAsACQAdQBpAFQAZQB4AHQALABbAFQAZQB4AHQALgBVAFQARgA4AEUAbgBjAG8AZABpAG4AZwBdADoAOgBuAGUAdwAoACQAZgBhAGwAcwBlACkAKQAKAA==

Add-Type -AssemblyName System.Windows.Forms
Add-Type -AssemblyName System.Drawing
[System.Windows.Forms.Application]::EnableVisualStyles()
$ErrorActionPreference='Stop'
$InstallerVersion='MiniAI v5.0.0'
$Self=$env:MINIAI_SELF
$installerDrive = if (![string]::IsNullOrWhiteSpace($Self)) { [IO.Path]::GetPathRoot($Self) } else { 'D:\' }
if ($installerDrive -ieq 'C:\') { $script:Root = Join-Path $env:ProgramFiles 'MiniAI' } else { $script:Root = Join-Path $installerDrive 'MiniAI' }
if (![string]::IsNullOrWhiteSpace($env:MINIAI_ROOT)) { $script:Root = [IO.Path]::GetFullPath($env:MINIAI_ROOT) }
$cacheParent = if ([IO.Path]::GetPathRoot($script:Root) -ieq 'C:\') { Join-Path $env:LOCALAPPDATA 'MiniAI_InstallerCache' } else { Split-Path -Parent $script:Root }
$Temp=Join-Path (Join-Path $cacheParent 'MiniAI_InstallerCache') ('Session_' + [guid]::NewGuid().ToString('N'))
New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $Temp -Force | Out-Null
$PayloadZip=Join-Path $Temp 'MiniAI_v5_payload.zip'
$PayloadDir=Join-Path $Temp 'MiniAI_v5_payload'
$Log=Join-Path $Temp 'MiniAI_Installer_v5.log'
$Err=Join-Path $Temp 'MiniAI_Installer_v5_error.txt'
$UpdateJson=Join-Path $Temp 'MiniAI_update.json'
$UpdateErr=Join-Path $Temp 'MiniAI_update_error.txt'
$global:UpdateAvailable=$false
$script:InstallStarting=$false
$script:WorkerLaunched=$false
function Log([string]$m){$line="[$(Get-Date -Format 'yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss')] $m";Add-Content -LiteralPath $Log -Value $line; if($status){$status.Text=$m}; }
function Extract-Payload{
  try{
    Log 'Preparing MiniAI v5 payload...'
    if([string]::IsNullOrWhiteSpace($Self) -or !(Test-Path -LiteralPath $Self)){throw ('Installer source file is missing: '+$Self)}
    $raw=[IO.File]::ReadAllText($Self,[Text.Encoding]::UTF8)
    $m='__MINIAI_PAYLOAD_BEGIN_7F3A91D2__'
    $i=$raw.LastIndexOf($m,[StringComparison]::Ordinal)
    if($i -lt 0){throw 'Embedded payload marker was not found in this installer file.'}
    $b=$raw.Substring($i+$m.Length).Trim()
    # Only the Base64 payload is allowed after the marker; ignore whitespace and reject other characters clearly.
    $b=$b -replace '\s',''
    if([string]::IsNullOrWhiteSpace($b)){throw 'Embedded payload is empty.'}
    if($b -notmatch '^[A-Za-z0-9+/]*={0,2}$'){throw 'The embedded payload contains non-Base64 characters. The installer file may be damaged or truncated.'}
    $bytes=[Convert]::FromBase64String($b)
    if($bytes.Length -lt 1000000){throw ('Embedded payload is unexpectedly small ('+$bytes.Length+' bytes).')}
    Add-Type -AssemblyName System.IO.Compression.FileSystem
    $requiredFiles=@('MiniAI_UI.exe','MiniAI_Launcher.exe','MiniAI_Flux2_Bridge.exe','Upgrade_MiniAI_v5.ps1','Upgrade_MiniAI_v5_Worker.ps1')
    $existingPayloadOK=$true
    foreach($required in $requiredFiles){if(!(Test-Path -LiteralPath (Join-Path $PayloadDir $required))){$existingPayloadOK=$false;break}}
    if($existingPayloadOK){Log 'Existing extracted payload is complete; reusing it without deleting files used by another process.';return $true}
    # Extract into a unique staging directory first; publish it only after ZIP and required-file checks pass.
    $stage=$PayloadDir+'.stage_'+[guid]::NewGuid().ToString('N')
    [IO.File]::WriteAllBytes($PayloadZip,$bytes)
    $zipCheck=[IO.Compression.ZipFile]::OpenRead($PayloadZip)
    try {if($zipCheck.Entries.Count -lt 7){throw ('Embedded payload ZIP has only '+$zipCheck.Entries.Count+' entries; expected at least 7.')} } finally {$zipCheck.Dispose()}
    [IO.Compression.ZipFile]::ExtractToDirectory($PayloadZip,$stage)
    foreach($required in $requiredFiles){if(!(Test-Path -LiteralPath (Join-Path $stage $required))){throw ('Payload ZIP is missing required file: '+$required)}}
    if(Test-Path -LiteralPath $PayloadDir){throw 'An incomplete payload directory already exists and may be in use. Close other MiniAI installer windows and retry; existing files were not deleted.'}
    Move-Item -LiteralPath $stage -Destination $PayloadDir -ErrorAction Stop
    Log ('Payload ready ('+$bytes.Length+' bytes; required files checked).')
    return $true
  }catch{
    $detail=($_ | Out-String)
    try{Set-Content -LiteralPath $Err -Value $detail -Encoding UTF8 -Force}catch{}
    try{Log ('PAYLOAD EXTRACTION ERROR: '+$_.Exception.Message)}catch{}
    try{[Windows.Forms.MessageBox]::Show(('MiniAI could not extract its embedded files.'+[Environment]::NewLine+[Environment]::NewLine+$_.Exception.Message+[Environment]::NewLine+[Environment]::NewLine+'Click More Details to open the diagnostic report.'),'MiniAI v5.0.0 - Payload Error',[Windows.Forms.MessageBoxButtons]::OK,[Windows.Forms.MessageBoxIcon]::Error)|Out-Null}catch{}
    return $false
  }
}
function Check-Updates{
  $global:UpdateAvailable=$false
  $updateButton.Text='Update The Installer'
  $updateButton.Enabled=$false
  $status.Text='Ready to install MiniAI.'
}
function Specific-Installation{
  [Windows.Forms.MessageBox]::Show('Choose the installation folder, then select Install or Fresh Install.','MiniAI',[Windows.Forms.MessageBoxButtons]::OK,[Windows.Forms.MessageBoxIcon]::Information) | Out-Null
}
function Start-Install([bool]$Fresh){
  if($script:InstallStarting){Log 'An installation attempt is already being prepared. Please wait for it to finish before clicking again.';return}
  $script:InstallStarting=$true;$script:WorkerLaunched=$false
  $script:Root=[IO.Path]::GetFullPath($script:Root).TrimEnd('\')
  $driveRoot=[IO.Path]::GetPathRoot($script:Root).TrimEnd('\')
  if([string]::IsNullOrWhiteSpace($script:Root) -or $script:Root -ieq $driveRoot -or $script:Root -ieq $env:WINDIR -or $script:Root -ieq $env:ProgramFiles -or $script:Root -ieq $env:LOCALAPPDATA){
    [Windows.Forms.MessageBox]::Show('Choose a dedicated MiniAI folder, not a drive root or a Windows system folder.','Invalid install folder') | Out-Null
    $script:InstallStarting=$false; return
  }
  $env:MINIAI_ROOT=$script:Root
  # Avoid stale-process false positives. Only block when the status file proves a recent active worker.
  if($script:ActiveUpgradeStatus -and (Test-Path -LiteralPath $script:ActiveUpgradeStatus)){
    try {
      $activeState=Get-Content -LiteralPath $script:ActiveUpgradeStatus -Raw|ConvertFrom-Json
      $updated=$null
      if($activeState.UpdatedAt){try{$updated=[datetime]::Parse([string]$activeState.UpdatedAt)}catch{}}
      $freshStatus=($updated -and ((Get-Date)-$updated).TotalMinutes -lt 3)
      if($activeState.Running -eq $true -and $freshStatus){Log 'An installation worker is actively running. Wait for its progress to finish before starting another.';$script:InstallStarting=$false;return}
    } catch {}
  }
  $script:UpgradeWindowProcess=$null
  try{
    $installButton.Enabled=$false;$freshButton.Enabled=$false;$uninstallButton.Enabled=$false;$updateButton.Enabled=$false;$progress.Visible=$true;$progress.Style='Continuous';$progress.Value=5
    if($Fresh){
      Log 'Fresh install selected. Removing the existing MiniAI installation folder as requested...';$progress.Value=10
      Get-Process MiniAI,MiniAI_UI,MiniAI_Core_v4,MiniAI_Flux2_Bridge -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue | Stop-Process -Force -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
      Start-Sleep -Milliseconds 700
      if(Test-Path -LiteralPath $script:Root){Remove-Item -LiteralPath $script:Root -Recurse -Force -ErrorAction Stop}
      if([IO.Path]::GetPathRoot($script:Root) -ieq 'C:\'){$dataRoot=Join-Path $env:LOCALAPPDATA 'MiniAI\Data';if(Test-Path -LiteralPath $dataRoot){Remove-Item -LiteralPath $dataRoot -Recurse -Force -ErrorAction Stop}}
      New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $script:Root -Force | Out-Null
      Log 'Fresh installation folder is ready.'
    }
    if(!(Test-Path -LiteralPath $script:Root)){New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $script:Root -Force|Out-Null}
    if(!(Extract-Payload)){throw 'MiniAI v5 payload extraction failed. Click More Details for the full console error.'}
    $progress.Value=35;Log 'Opening the separate MiniAI v5 upgrade window...'
    $up=Join-Path $PayloadDir 'Upgrade_MiniAI_v5.ps1'
    $upgradeStatus=Join-Path $Temp 'MiniAI_v5_upgrade_status.json'
    $upgradeLog=Join-Path $Temp 'MiniAI_v5_upgrade.log'
    $script:ActiveUpgradeStatus=$upgradeStatus
    if(Test-Path -LiteralPath $upgradeStatus){Remove-Item -LiteralPath $upgradeStatus -Force}
    $env:MINIAI_ROOT=$script:Root
    $arguments='-NoLogo -NoProfile -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -STA -File "'+$up+'" -StatusFile "'+$upgradeStatus+'" -LogFile "'+$upgradeLog+'"'
    $pr=Start-Process -FilePath 'powershell.exe' -ArgumentList $arguments -WorkingDirectory $PayloadDir -WindowStyle Normal -PassThru
    $script:UpgradeWindowProcess=$pr;$script:WorkerLaunched=$true
    $progress.Value=100;Log ('Upgrade progress window opened (PID '+$pr.Id+'). The actual download and installation run in a separate worker process. Watch the live progress there; the main installer remains available.');$script:MainUpgradeTimer.Start()
  }catch{$_ | Out-String | Set-Content -LiteralPath $Err -Encoding UTF8;Log ('ERROR: '+$_.Exception.Message);$progress.Visible=$false;$script:InstallStarting=$false;$installButton.Enabled=$true;$freshButton.Enabled=$true;$uninstallButton.Enabled=$true;$updateButton.Enabled=$global:UpdateAvailable}
  finally{if(!$script:WorkerLaunched){$script:InstallStarting=$false;$installButton.Enabled=$true;$freshButton.Enabled=$true;$uninstallButton.Enabled=$true;$updateButton.Enabled=$global:UpdateAvailable}}
}
function Uninstall-MiniAI{
  if([Windows.Forms.MessageBox]::Show(('Permanently remove MiniAI and all files inside '+$script:Root+'? This does not affect files outside that folder.'),'Uninstall MiniAI',[Windows.Forms.MessageBoxButtons]::YesNo,[Windows.Forms.MessageBoxIcon]::Warning) -eq 'Yes'){
    Get-Process MiniAI,MiniAI_UI,MiniAI_Core_v4,MiniAI_Flux2_Bridge -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue | Stop-Process -Force -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
    Start-Sleep -Milliseconds 500
    if(Test-Path -LiteralPath $script:Root){Remove-Item -LiteralPath $script:Root -Recurse -Force -ErrorAction Stop}
    if([IO.Path]::GetPathRoot($script:Root) -ieq 'C:\'){$dataRoot=Join-Path $env:LOCALAPPDATA 'MiniAI\Data';if(Test-Path -LiteralPath $dataRoot){Remove-Item -LiteralPath $dataRoot -Recurse -Force -ErrorAction Stop}}
    Log 'MiniAI installation folder removed.'
    $freshButton.Visible=$false; $uninstallButton.Visible=$false
  }
}
function Update-Installer{
  Start-Process 'https://github.com/Kelphal/MiniAI/releases'
}
function More-Details{
  try{
    $report=Join-Path (Split-Path -Parent $Log) 'MiniAI_v5_diagnostics.txt'
    $parts=New-Object System.Collections.Generic.List[string]
    $parts.Add('MiniAI v5.0.0 - Diagnostic report')
    $parts.Add(('Created: '+(Get-Date)))
    $parts.Add(('Installer: '+$Self))
    $parts.Add(('Install directory: '+$script:Root))
    $parts.Add(('Payload ZIP: '+$PayloadZip))
    $parts.Add(('Payload directory: '+$PayloadDir))
    $parts.Add(('Installer session: '+$Temp))
    $parts.Add('Concurrent install attempts are blocked while a worker is active.')
    $parts.Add('')
    $parts.Add('===== Installer log =====')
    if(Test-Path -LiteralPath $Log){$parts.AddRange([string[]](Get-Content -LiteralPath $Log -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue))}else{$parts.Add('No installer log found.')}
    $parts.Add('')
    $parts.Add('===== Last error / payload extraction error =====')
    if(Test-Path -LiteralPath $Err){$parts.AddRange([string[]](Get-Content -LiteralPath $Err -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue))}else{$parts.Add('No error report found.')}
    $parts.Add('')
    $parts.Add('===== Upgrade log =====')
    $ul=Join-Path $Temp 'MiniAI_v5_upgrade.log'
    if(Test-Path -LiteralPath $ul){$parts.AddRange([string[]](Get-Content -LiteralPath $ul -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue))}else{$parts.Add('No upgrade log found.')}
    $parts.Add('')
    $parts.Add('===== Image generator model files =====')
    $model=Join-Path $script:Root 'Models\FLUX2'
    if(Test-Path -LiteralPath $model){$parts.AddRange([string[]](Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $model -Force -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue | Select-Object -ExpandProperty Name))}else{$parts.Add('Models\FLUX2 not found.')}
    Set-Content -LiteralPath $report -Value $parts.ToArray() -Encoding UTF8
    Start-Process -FilePath 'notepad.exe' -ArgumentList ('"'+$report+'"') -ErrorAction Stop
  }catch{[Windows.Forms.MessageBox]::Show(('Could not open diagnostics: '+$_.Exception.Message),'MiniAI Diagnostics')|Out-Null}
}
$form=New-Object Windows.Forms.Form;$form.Text='MiniAI Setup';$form.Size=New-Object Drawing.Size(720,560);$form.StartPosition='CenterScreen';$form.FormBorderStyle='FixedDialog';$form.MaximizeBox=$false;$form.MinimizeBox=$true;$form.BackColor=[Drawing.Color]::FromArgb(12,14,19);$form.UseWaitCursor=$false;$form.Cursor=[Windows.Forms.Cursors]::Default
$header=New-Object Windows.Forms.Panel;$header.BackColor=[Drawing.Color]::FromArgb(8,12,20);$header.Location=New-Object Drawing.Point(0,0);$header.Size=New-Object Drawing.Size(704,92);$form.Controls.Add($header)
$header.Add_Paint({$brush=New-Object Drawing.Drawing2D.LinearGradientBrush($header.ClientRectangle,[Drawing.Color]::FromArgb(46,35,12),[Drawing.Color]::FromArgb(8,12,20),0);try{$_.Graphics.FillRectangle($brush,$header.ClientRectangle)}finally{$brush.Dispose()}})

$status=New-Object Windows.Forms.Label;$status.Text='Starting...';$status.ForeColor=[Drawing.Color]::White;$status.Location=New-Object Drawing.Point(26,108);$status.Size=New-Object Drawing.Size(650,32);$form.Controls.Add($status)
$progress=New-Object Windows.Forms.ProgressBar;$progress.Location=New-Object Drawing.Point(26,143);$progress.Size=New-Object Drawing.Size(650,24);$progress.Style='Continuous';$progress.Value=0;$progress.ForeColor=[Drawing.Color]::FromArgb(0,122,255);$progress.Visible=$false;$form.Controls.Add($progress)
$installButton=New-Object Windows.Forms.Button;$installButton.Text='Install / Upgrade MiniAI + Image Generator';$installButton.Location=New-Object Drawing.Point(26,185);$installButton.Size=New-Object Drawing.Size(315,42);$form.Controls.Add($installButton)
$freshButton=New-Object Windows.Forms.Button;$freshButton.Text='Fresh Install';$freshButton.Enabled=$true;$freshButton.Location=New-Object Drawing.Point(361,185);$freshButton.Size=New-Object Drawing.Size(315,42);$form.Controls.Add($freshButton)
$uninstallButton=New-Object Windows.Forms.Button;$uninstallButton.Text='Uninstall';$uninstallButton.Enabled=$true;$uninstallButton.Location=New-Object Drawing.Point(26,237);$uninstallButton.Size=New-Object Drawing.Size(315,42);$form.Controls.Add($uninstallButton)

$detailsButton=New-Object Windows.Forms.Button;$detailsButton.Text='More Details';$detailsButton.Location=New-Object Drawing.Point(26,292);$detailsButton.Size=New-Object Drawing.Size(315,40);$form.Controls.Add($detailsButton)
$specificButton=New-Object Windows.Forms.Button;$specificButton.Text='Choose a v5+ Release (Optional)';$specificButton.Location=New-Object Drawing.Point(361,292);$specificButton.Size=New-Object Drawing.Size(315,40);$form.Controls.Add($specificButton)
$closeButton=New-Object Windows.Forms.Button;$closeButton.Text='Close';$closeButton.Location=New-Object Drawing.Point(580,348);$closeButton.Size=New-Object Drawing.Size(96,34);$form.Controls.Add($closeButton)
$note=New-Object Windows.Forms.Label;$note.Text='MiniAI v5.0.0';$note.ForeColor=[Drawing.Color]::LightSteelBlue;$note.Location=New-Object Drawing.Point(26,355);$note.AutoSize=$true;$form.Controls.Add($note)
$imageGeneratorNote=New-Object Windows.Forms.Label;$imageGeneratorNote.Text='Included automatically: integrated image generator (~2.3 GB model files; no browser setup needed)';$imageGeneratorNote.Location=New-Object Drawing.Point(26,389);$imageGeneratorNote.Size=New-Object Drawing.Size(650,28);$imageGeneratorNote.ForeColor=[Drawing.Color]::LightSteelBlue;$form.Controls.Add($imageGeneratorNote)
$script:ActiveUpgradeStatus=$null
$script:UpgradeWindowProcess=$null
$script:MainUpgradeTimer=New-Object Windows.Forms.Timer;$script:MainUpgradeTimer.Interval=700
$script:MainUpgradeTimer.Add_Tick({
  if(!$script:ActiveUpgradeStatus){return}
  if(!(Test-Path -LiteralPath $script:ActiveUpgradeStatus)){
    if($script:UpgradeWindowProcess){try{if($script:UpgradeWindowProcess.HasExited){$status.Text='Upgrade window closed before the install started. Click Install / Upgrade MiniAI + Image Generator to open it again.';$progress.Visible=$false;$script:MainUpgradeTimer.Stop();$script:InstallStarting=$false;$script:WorkerLaunched=$false;$installButton.Enabled=$true;$freshButton.Enabled=$true;$uninstallButton.Enabled=$true;return}}catch{}}
    $status.Text='Integrated installer opened. Click Install / Upgrade MiniAI + Image Generator to begin.';$progress.Visible=$false;return
  }
  try{
    $st=Get-Content -LiteralPath $script:ActiveUpgradeStatus -Raw|ConvertFrom-Json
    $status.Text=[string]$st.Message
    $progress.Visible=$true
    if([int]$st.Percent -ge 0){$progress.Style='Continuous';$progress.Value=[int][Math]::Max(0,[Math]::Min(100,[int]$st.Percent))}else{$progress.Style='Marquee'}
    if([long]$st.TotalBytes -gt 0){$status.Text+='  ('+[math]::Round(([double]$st.DownloadedBytes/1GB),2)+' / '+[math]::Round(([double]$st.TotalBytes/1GB),2)+' GB)'}
    if($st.Running -eq $false){if($st.Success -eq $true){$progress.Style='Continuous';$progress.Value=100;$status.Text='MiniAI v5 installation completed. '+[string]$st.Message}else{$status.Text='Install error: '+[string]$st.Message};$script:MainUpgradeTimer.Stop();$script:InstallStarting=$false;$script:WorkerLaunched=$false;$installButton.Enabled=$true;$freshButton.Enabled=$true;$uninstallButton.Enabled=$true;$updateButton.Enabled=$global:UpdateAvailable}
  }catch{}
})
$installButton.Add_Click({Start-Install $false});$freshButton.Add_Click({if([Windows.Forms.MessageBox]::Show(('Fresh Install permanently removes the existing files inside '+$script:Root+' and installs a clean copy. Continue?'),'Fresh Install',[Windows.Forms.MessageBoxButtons]::YesNo,[Windows.Forms.MessageBoxIcon]::Warning) -eq 'Yes'){Start-Install $true}});$uninstallButton.Add_Click({Uninstall-MiniAI});$updateButton.Add_Click({if($global:UpdateAvailable){Update-Installer}});$detailsButton.Add_Click({More-Details});$specificButton.Add_Click({Specific-Installation});$closeButton.Add_Click({$form.Close()})
$freshButton.Visible=$true;$uninstallButton.Visible=$true
